# Errata register - PD-RETAIL v4

Rule errata take effect only when the latest status of their change request in the governance CR register is APPROVED. Row errata correct one reported DPD value; only rows with status APPLIED take effect and, for the same account and period, the row with the later raised date wins (ties by erratum id).

## Rule errata

| erratum | document | change | change request |
|---|---|---|---|
| E-002 | model_documentation_v4.pdf | MORT cut point c5 reads 805; the correct value is 812 | CR-93494 |
| E-004 | bad_definition_standard.docx 2.1 | the DPD threshold for BNPL is 60 | CR-16938 |
| E-006 | model_documentation_v4.pdf | CARD cut point c3 reads 720; proposed value 711 | CR-59966 |
| E-009 | bad_definition_standard.docx 2.1 | the DPD threshold for OVDR is 60 | CR-79460 |
| E-011 | exclusion_policy.docx 2.2 | the to date of a litigation hold is inclusive | CR-21933 |
